# OPEN ITEMS REGISTER — the ONE home for every discovered-but-unresolved issue

> **Created 2026-07-10 (session 36), user-directed** after a full-repo sweep found 91 open items
> scattered across 12 tracking surfaces with 11 status contradictions. **Split into a lean INDEX +
> per-item detail files 2026-07-26 (user-ratified option 1):** this file is the complete INDEX and
> the **authoritative status surface**; every item's full original row (text + source + status)
> lives verbatim in `open_items/OI-<n>.md`, which carries narrative and provenance only and never a
> status of its own. **Standing rule (CLAUDE.md "Open-items register" section):** read this INDEX at
> session start; a stage may not open while a register item gating it is open; every new discovery
> gets an index row AND its detail file in the same commit that records it; every resolution flips
> its INDEX row with provenance (the detail file gains a dated resolution note, never a status of
> record). Detail lives in `open_items/`; rows here are pointers, not restatements. IDs are stable;
> do not renumber.
>
> **★ EVERY STATUS CELL BEGINS WITH ONE CANONICAL TOKEN** — the resolved mark at the head of the
> cell, or one of the open-state words. A row's state is read from that token and never by searching
> its prose, so a cell may name any other row's resolution freely, and a resolution spelled only in
> words is not a state. **This line is a POINTER, not the rule's home:** the vocabulary and the check
> are `tools/audit/index_status_lint.py`, which STOPs on a non-canonical opening, and the ONE index
> parser STOPs on a row that does not split into six cells. Ruled by the user 2026-08-09 (Ruling 33
> of `cowork_rulings_2026_08_09_fifth_stop.md`) over the family [[OI-356]] / [[OI-361]] / [[OI-362]].

> **★★ GOVERNING ARCHITECTURE DECISION (user-ratified 2026-07-17) — read `cowork_joint_estimator_architecture.md`.**
> The key/mode/chord estimator is **JOINT (option A)** — one probabilistic decode over `(tonic, mode, chord)`
> with segmentation as a modeled (semi-Markov) variable and every enumerated clue as a theory-grounded factor;
> NOT a feed-forward pipeline, NOT a separable "key layer." Theory basis: `cowork_key_chord_joint_inference_grounding.md`.
> This **reframes many rows below as A-factors or superseded** (architecture doc §3): OI-166 → the L1.5 cadence
> factor; OI-168/OI-170 → the signature-mask collection emission term; OI-174/OI-147 → A's mode-vocabulary
> question; OI-175 → superseded by the joint decode; OI-23/OI-91/OI-97 (hand-set constants) → retire into A's
> fit-once factors; OI-145 wave-1 (the measurement chain) is RETAINED. **Immediate next action:** the
> term-level theory-grounding audit (READ-ONLY, the #17 funnel's first stage — enumerate every factor, derive
> each form from established theory #1/#2, triage keep/fix/drop). The estimator funnel stays SHUT until the
> forms are pinned and ratified; forms from theory, values fit ONCE against GT (#19), never per-case tuned
> (#8/DT-2). Full arc: `cowork_handoff.md` 2026-07-17 entry.

A pc-level or voice-deduplicated emission is a refit-requiring MODEL change, never a knob-turn. Every other read of working-tree content went through Read, Grep and Glob. **NOT in doubt:** no analysis, no analysis input, no corpus of scores, no measurement, no golden, nothing in `tools/robust_stop/`, no entry or status in either register; the `git status` denial shows the hook working on the shape it was measured against, and none of the widening's own measured rows is questioned | D — instrument / measurement layer (the audit's own guard apparatus) | ✅ RESOLVED 2026-08-08 (CC, `cc_instruction_away_execution.md` Task 1), inside the one family design as Ruling 3 of `cowork_rulings_2026_08_08_pre_away.md` requires, and never as its own patch. **★ THE CAUSE IS ESTABLISHED AND IT IS THE FIRST CANDIDATE, IN A FORM THE ROW DID NOT SUPPOSE: THE DRIVE LETTER'S CASE.** `outside_repo` compared `os.path.commonpath(...)` against `ROOT` with `!=` — a **case-sensitive** string comparison on a platform whose paths are case-insensitive — and `ntpath.commonpath` takes the drive from the token AS WRITTEN, so a path spelled `c:/s/MS/…` produced `c:\s\MS`, which is not the string `C:\s\MS`, and read as OUTSIDE the repository. `os.path.normcase` on both sides is the fix; on POSIX it is the identity, so nothing moves there. **★ AND IT IS NOT AN `ls` DEFECT:** the corpus now carries `cat` and `grep` — the guard's oldest vocabulary — aimed at repository files in the same spelling, and at the unwidened guard **both were admitted**. non-option token, the same correction the four pattern-taking utilities already carried, one utility class further out. **BOTH RATES RE-MEASURED ON THE SAME EXTENDED CORPUS, so the comparison is of Python makes absolute against the process's own current directory, so the DRIVE LETTER'S CASE arrived in whatever spelling the invocation used — the FAMILY arm normcases both sides and never saw it, ## I. Siloed analyzed facts (fact-publication sweep 2026-07-10 — `cowork_siloed_facts_audit.md`)

One root cause, one design owner: the shared cross-layer surfaces are voice-blind,
spelling-blind, membership-blind — resolved by the **E4 carry/surface design** (which facts the
shared surfaces publish), not per-site patches. OI-15 (spelling) is the headline instance.
